# Crafting Apps

[![Build Status](https://img.shields.io/github/actions/workflow/status/daemonless/crafting-apps/build.yaml?style=flat-square&label=Build&color=green)](https://github.com/daemonless/crafting-apps/actions)
[![Last Commit](https://img.shields.io/github/last-commit/daemonless/crafting-apps?style=flat-square&label=Last+Commit&color=blue)](https://github.com/daemonless/crafting-apps/commits)

ArtCraft's seven open-source, clean-room Adobe alternatives (PhotoCraft, VectorCraft, FilmCraft, LightCraft, PdfCraft, EffectCraft, DesignCraft) as WebAssembly web apps. Pick the ones you want.

| | |
|---|---|
| **Port** | 8080 |
| **Registry** | `ghcr.io/daemonless/crafting-apps` |
| **Source** | [https://github.com/storytold](https://github.com/storytold) |
| **Website** | [https://getartcraft.com/apps](https://getartcraft.com/apps) |

## Parts

| Service | Image | Role | |
|---|---|---|---|
| **photocraft** | `ghcr.io/daemonless/photocraft:latest` | Image editor (Photoshop) | [docs](https://github.com/daemonless/photocraft) |
| **vectorcraft** | `ghcr.io/daemonless/vectorcraft:latest` | Vector illustration (Illustrator) | [docs](https://github.com/daemonless/vectorcraft) |
| **filmcraft** | `ghcr.io/daemonless/filmcraft:latest` | Video editor (Premiere Pro) | [docs](https://github.com/daemonless/filmcraft) |
| **lightcraft** | `ghcr.io/daemonless/lightcraft:latest` | Photo library and raw developer (Lightroom) | [docs](https://github.com/daemonless/lightcraft) |
| **pdfcraft** | `ghcr.io/daemonless/pdfcraft:latest` | PDF workbench (Acrobat) | [docs](https://github.com/daemonless/pdfcraft) |
| **effectcraft** | `ghcr.io/daemonless/effectcraft:latest` | Motion graphics and effects (After Effects) | [docs](https://github.com/daemonless/effectcraft) |
| **designcraft** | `ghcr.io/daemonless/designcraft:latest` | Page layout (InDesign) | [docs](https://github.com/daemonless/designcraft) |

## Prerequisites
Before deploying, ensure your host environment is ready. See the [Quick Start Guide](https://daemonless.io/guides/quick-start) for host setup instructions.

## Deployment

### Podman Compose

**PhotoCraft.** Image editing with layers, masks, adjustment layers and PSD files (Photoshop).

**VectorCraft.** Vector illustration (Illustrator).

**FilmCraft.** Video editing (Premiere Pro).

**LightCraft.** Photo library and raw developer (Lightroom).

**PdfCraft.** Read, organize, combine, split and secure PDFs (Acrobat).

**EffectCraft.** Motion graphics and visual effects (After Effects).

**DesignCraft.** Page layout and publishing (InDesign).

#### Included, Included, Included, Included, Included, Included, Included (default)

**1.** Save as `.env` and fill in what is empty:

```env { data-zip-bundle="crafting-apps-podman" data-zip-filename=".env" }
# Crafting Apps for FreeBSD (Daemonless)

# The user and group the nginx workers run as.
PUID=1000
PGID=1000

# To set a timezone, change Etc/UTC to a TZ identifier
# https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_tz_database_time_zones#List
TZ=Etc/UTC
```

**2.** Save as `compose.yaml`:

```yaml { data-zip-bundle="crafting-apps-podman" data-zip-filename="compose.yaml" }
#
# Crafting Apps for FreeBSD (Daemonless)
#
# ArtCraft's seven open-source creative apps, each a static WebAssembly site
# served by nginx. Pick the ones you want; nothing is shared between them.
#
# Usage:
#   1. Copy example.env to .env and edit
#   2. Run: podman-compose up -d
#

name: crafting-apps

services:
  photocraft:
    container_name: photocraft
    image: ghcr.io/daemonless/photocraft:latest
    environment:
      PUID: ${PUID:-1000}
      PGID: ${PGID:-1000}
      TZ: ${TZ:-UTC}
    ports:
      - "8080:80"
    restart: always

  vectorcraft:
    container_name: vectorcraft
    image: ghcr.io/daemonless/vectorcraft:latest
    environment:
      PUID: ${PUID:-1000}
      PGID: ${PGID:-1000}
      TZ: ${TZ:-UTC}
    ports:
      - "8081:80"
    restart: always

  filmcraft:
    container_name: filmcraft
    image: ghcr.io/daemonless/filmcraft:latest
    environment:
      PUID: ${PUID:-1000}
      PGID: ${PGID:-1000}
      TZ: ${TZ:-UTC}
    ports:
      - "8082:80"
    restart: always

  lightcraft:
    container_name: lightcraft
    image: ghcr.io/daemonless/lightcraft:latest
    environment:
      PUID: ${PUID:-1000}
      PGID: ${PGID:-1000}
      TZ: ${TZ:-UTC}
    ports:
      - "8083:80"
    restart: always

  pdfcraft:
    container_name: pdfcraft
    image: ghcr.io/daemonless/pdfcraft:latest
    environment:
      PUID: ${PUID:-1000}
      PGID: ${PGID:-1000}
      TZ: ${TZ:-UTC}
    ports:
      - "8084:80"
    restart: always

  effectcraft:
    container_name: effectcraft
    image: ghcr.io/daemonless/effectcraft:latest
    environment:
      PUID: ${PUID:-1000}
      PGID: ${PGID:-1000}
      TZ: ${TZ:-UTC}
    ports:
      - "8085:80"
    restart: always

  designcraft:
    container_name: designcraft
    image: ghcr.io/daemonless/designcraft:latest
    environment:
      PUID: ${PUID:-1000}
      PGID: ${PGID:-1000}
      TZ: ${TZ:-UTC}
    ports:
      - "8086:80"
    restart: always
```

Then run `podman-compose up -d`.

```yaml { data-zip-bundle="crafting-apps-podman-designcraft-off" data-zip-filename="compose.yaml" }
name: crafting-apps

    image: ghcr.io/daemonless/photocraft:latest
    environment:
      PUID: ${PUID:-1000}
      PGID: ${PGID:-1000}
      TZ: ${TZ:-UTC}
    ports:
      - "8080:80"
    restart: always

  vectorcraft:
    container_name: vectorcraft
    image: ghcr.io/daemonless/vectorcraft:latest

      PUID: ${PUID:-1000}
```

```env { data-zip-bundle="crafting-apps-podman-effectcraft-off" data-zip-filename=".env" }
# Crafting Apps for FreeBSD (Daemonless)
```
```yaml { data-zip-bundle="crafting-apps-podman-effectcraft-off" data-zip-filename="compose.yaml" }
#
# ArtCraft's seven open-source creative apps, each a static WebAssembly site
# served by nginx. Pick the ones you want; nothing is shared between them.
#
# Usage:
#   1. Copy example.env to .env and edit
#   2. Run: podman-compose up -d
#

name: crafting-apps

services:
  photocraft:
    container_name: photocraft
    image: ghcr.io/daemonless/photocraft:latest
    environment:
      PUID: ${PUID:-1000}
      PGID: ${PGID:-1000}
      TZ: ${TZ:-UTC}
    ports:
      - "8080:80"
    restart: always

  filmcraft:
    container_name: filmcraft
    image: ghcr.io/daemonless/filmcraft:latest
    environment:
      PUID: ${PUID:-1000}
      PGID: ${PGID:-1000}
      TZ: ${TZ:-UTC}
    ports:
      - "8082:80"
    restart: always

  pdfcraft:
    container_name: pdfcraft
    image: ghcr.io/daemonless/pdfcraft:latest
    environment:
      PUID: ${PUID:-1000}
      PGID: ${PGID:-1000}
      TZ: ${TZ:-UTC}
    ports:
      - "8084:80"
    restart: always
```

```yaml { data-zip-bundle="crafting-apps-podman-pdfcraft-off" data-zip-filename="compose.yaml" }
  effectcraft:
    container_name: effectcraft
    image: ghcr.io/daemonless/effectcraft:latest
    environment:
      PUID: ${PUID:-1000}
      PGID: ${PGID:-1000}
      TZ: ${TZ:-UTC}
    ports:
      - "8085:80"
    restart: always

  designcraft:
    container_name: designcraft
    image: ghcr.io/daemonless/designcraft:latest
    environment:
      PUID: ${PUID:-1000}
      PGID: ${PGID:-1000}
      TZ: ${TZ:-UTC}
    ports:
      - "8086:80"
    restart: always
```

#### Included, Included, Included, Included, Included, Included, Included (default)

**appjail-director.yml**:

```yaml
# appjail-director.yml

options:
- virtualnet: :<random> default
- nat:
services:
  photocraft:
    name: photocraft
    options:
    - container: boot args:--pull=newer
    - expose: 8080:80 proto:tcp
    - from: ghcr.io/daemonless/photocraft:latest
    oci:
      user: root
      environment:
      - PUID: !ENV '${PUID}'
      - PGID: !ENV '${PGID}'
      - TZ: !ENV '${TZ}'
  vectorcraft:
    name: vectorcraft
    options:
    - container: boot args:--pull=newer
    - expose: 8081:80 proto:tcp
    - from: ghcr.io/daemonless/vectorcraft:latest
    oci:
      user: root
      environment:
      - PUID: !ENV '${PUID}'
      - PGID: !ENV '${PGID}'
      - TZ: !ENV '${TZ}'
  filmcraft:
    name: filmcraft
    options:
    - container: boot args:--pull=newer
    - expose: 8082:80 proto:tcp
    - from: ghcr.io/daemonless/filmcraft:latest
    oci:
      user: root
      environment:
      - PUID: !ENV '${PUID}'
      - PGID: !ENV '${PGID}'
      - TZ: !ENV '${TZ}'
  lightcraft:
    name: lightcraft
    options:
    - container: boot args:--pull=newer
    - expose: 8083:80 proto:tcp
    - from: ghcr.io/daemonless/lightcraft:latest
    oci:
      user: root
      environment:
      - PUID: !ENV '${PUID}'
      - PGID: !ENV '${PGID}'
      - TZ: !ENV '${TZ}'
  pdfcraft:
    name: pdfcraft
    options:
    - container: boot args:--pull=newer
    - expose: 8084:80 proto:tcp
    - from: ghcr.io/daemonless/pdfcraft:latest
    oci:
      user: root
      environment:
      - PUID: !ENV '${PUID}'
      - PGID: !ENV '${PGID}'
      - TZ: !ENV '${TZ}'
  effectcraft:
    name: effectcraft
    options:
    - container: boot args:--pull=newer
    - expose: 8085:80 proto:tcp
    - from: ghcr.io/daemonless/effectcraft:latest
    oci:
      user: root
      environment:
      - PUID: !ENV '${PUID}'
      - PGID: !ENV '${PGID}'
      - TZ: !ENV '${TZ}'
  designcraft:
    name: designcraft
    options:
    - container: boot args:--pull=newer
    - expose: 8086:80 proto:tcp
    - from: ghcr.io/daemonless/designcraft:latest
    oci:
      user: root
      environment:
      - PUID: !ENV '${PUID}'
      - PGID: !ENV '${PGID}'
      - TZ: !ENV '${TZ}'
```

Access at: `http://localhost:8080`

## Setup Instructions

**Everything runs in the browser.** Each app is Rust compiled to WebAssembly; the containers only serve static files. Files open through the browser's file picker and save as downloads, and nothing is stored server side, so there are no volumes.

**Your work lives in the browser, not on the server.** Preferences, LightCraft's photo library (in the browser's origin-private file system) and EffectCraft's offline cache belong to the browser profile that opened the app, per origin. Clearing site data, switching browsers, or reaching the app under a different hostname means starting empty. Export what you want to keep; the exported files are the backup, as with any desktop app.

**HTTPS.** WebGPU and the clipboard need a secure context. Over plain HTTP the apps fall back to WebGL2; put them behind your reverse proxy for the full experience.

**Fonts.** The browser build ships with only two fonts. PhotoCraft also lists the Japanese, Chinese and Arabic families from [craft-fonts](https://github.com/storytold/craft-fonts), which the photocraft image serves alongside the app; a family is downloaded the first time you pick it or open a document that uses it.

## Parameters

### Environment Variables

| Variable | Default | Description |
|----------|---------|-------------|
| `PUID` | `1000` | User ID for the nginx worker processes |
| `PGID` | `1000` | Group ID for the nginx worker processes |
| `TZ` | `Etc/UTC` | Timezone for the containers |

### Ports

| Port | Protocol | Description |
|------|----------|-------------|
| `80` | TCP |  |

**Architectures:** amd64, aarch64
**User:** `bsd` (UID/GID via PUID/PGID, defaults to 1000:1000)
**Base:** FreeBSD 15.1
